## Summary

The complete maintained thioredoxin record retains two native events, nine native side occurrences, a separate five-side Rhea projection of the same reductase event, 45 mechanistic edges, four qualified endpoints and 116 references. Complete independent science is inherited with explicit source/construct limits and the accepted TrxA/DsbD corrections. Fresh integration and rendered graph/table checks are separately identified.

### Original construct and experimental context CORE05

scope: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

Inherited independent context assessment. Native two-monomer complex and UniProt FAD evidence agree. Historical B, K12 recombinant and inferred native assembly contexts remain separate.

E. coli TrxB homodimer consists of two THIOREDOXIN-REDUCT-NADPH-MONOMER subunits. Native complex has no direct common-name; nested activity has thioredoxin reductase. One FAD per subunit and electron flow NADPH to FAD to TrxB disulfide to thioredoxin are supported. Native E. coli B purification, recombinant K12 studies and inferred K12 assembly history must remain distinguished.

### Original construct and experimental context M01

scope: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

Inherited independent context assessment. Primary abstract supports 139 residues, 32-residue extension, four extra cysteines, recombinant RNR/insulin activity, DTT effect and heat sensitivity; uninspected vector/tag details stay unresolved.

TrxC/Trx2 is a 139-residue second E. coli thioredoxin with an additional 32-residue N-terminal region and four additional cysteines. Recombinant Trx2 supports ribonucleotide reductase with TrxB and NADPH and reduces insulin disulfides. DTT preincubation enhances full-length Trx2 but not the N-terminal deletion; the N-terminal region contributes heat sensitivity. Expression vector/tag details are unresolved without the primary body.

### Original construct and experimental context M02

scope: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

Inherited independent context assessment. Full eight-page Collet paper read; pages3/7 also visually inspected. Twelve added contexts retain forcing conditions, zinc stoichiometry, untagged preparation, active-site versus metal-site distinction, negative assay limits and uncertain physiology.

TrxC binds one zinc per protein through its four N-terminal cysteines with high affinity; peroxide releases zinc with conformational change. Apo-Trx2 retains reductase activity. The physiological function of the zinc center remains unresolved: do not assert an established native redox switch.

Trx2 differs from Trx1 in redox potential and active-site pKa. A four zinc-cysteine-to-serine construct changes potential, pKa and thermostability. Native source also reports undefined high-mass complexes during oxidative stress. Retain the latter as source context with unidentified partners, not a named stable native assembly.

### Original construct and experimental context M04

scope: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

Oxidized OxyR binds trxC promoter and activates expression. TrxA and TrxC overlap in tested ribonucleotide reductase, PAPS reductase and DsbC-supporting functions; N-terminal TrxC extension is dispensable for these tests. Double-thioredoxin mutants are more peroxide-resistant yet diamide-sensitive. These context-dependent results do not prove complete equivalence across all substrates.

Inherited complete independent assessment: Freshly purified Trx2 contained six reduced cysteines and approximately one zinc atom per protein. ICP-HRMS gave 1.03 zinc/protein with no other tested metals detected. The four N-terminal Cys residues coordinate the metal; selective active-site oxidation by 10-fold GSSG at 4 °C for 10 min did not release zinc.

Retained restriction: The two catalytic cysteines and four zinc-binding cysteines are distinct; preserve one native catalytic disulfide versus three-disulfide experimental preparation. Original reviewer action, inherited rather than repeated here: Independently read original full Collet paper and relevant figure pages.

### Additional primary/structure context C03

scope: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

Inherited complete independent assessment: PAR alone could not extract zinc; thiol modification by PMPS released zinc and DTT restored binding. TPEN competition supports high affinity. EDTA was unsuitable for affinity competition because the authors report a Trx2-zinc-EDTA complex (data not shown).

Retained restriction: Do not report all chelators as equivalent or convert a failed competition method into an affinity measurement. Text and figure specify slightly different pH for the affinity experiments. Original reviewer action, inherited rather than repeated here: Independently read original full Collet paper and relevant figure pages.

### Additional primary/structure context C04

scope: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

Inherited complete independent assessment: Completely oxidized Trx2 was prepared with 40-fold excess H2O2 at 43 °C for 2 h. Figure4 compared 50 µM Trx2 with 2 mM H2O2 at 23 °C and 43 °C; zinc release and thiol oxidation were faster at 43 °C. Copper acceleration is described as data not shown.

Retained restriction: Forcing in vitro peroxide/temperature conditions; physiological zinc-center oxidation and a functional redox switch remain unproven. Original reviewer action, inherited rather than repeated here: Independently read original full Collet paper and relevant figure pages.

### Additional primary/structure context C05

scope: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

Inherited complete independent assessment: TrxB plus NADPH reduced fully oxidized Trx2, with NADPH consumption corresponding to all three disulfides. A slow two-cysteine phase preceded faster reduction of four remaining cysteines; assignment of the first pair to the catalytic cysteines was tentative. Zinc occupancy recovered to about25% without added zinc and about85% with added zinc.

Retained restriction: This auxiliary three-disulfide experiment does not change one-disulfide native Rhea20347 stoichiometry or prove direct NADPH catalysis by TrxC. Original reviewer action, inherited rather than repeated here: Independently read original full Collet paper and relevant figure pages.

### Additional primary/structure context C06

scope: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

Inherited complete independent assessment: Size-exclusion apparent mass shifted with oxidation, but analytical ultracentrifugation at 10 µM found both redox preparations monomeric (16,470±710 Da). SDS-PAGE showed &gt;80% monomer with a minor crosslinked dimer fraction reported as data not shown.

Retained restriction: Do not infer a stable native oligomer from native-gel bands or hydrodynamic mass. Original reviewer action, inherited rather than repeated here: Independently read original full Collet paper and relevant figure pages.

### Additional primary/structure context C07

scope: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

Inherited complete independent assessment: Oxidation changed far-UV CD and increased protease susceptibility. The interpretation as increased alpha-helical structure was tentative.

Retained restriction: A conformational change is supported; a complete experimentally resolved alternate structure is not. Original reviewer action, inherited rather than repeated here: Independently read original full Collet paper and relevant figure pages.

### Additional primary/structure context C08

scope: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

Inherited complete independent assessment: Reduced zinc-free Trx2 retained the same insulin reduction rate as zinc-bound reduced Trx2 under the DTT-driven assay (130 µM insulin, 0.6 mM DTT, 1 µM thioredoxin, pH7.0,25 °C). Both Trx2 preparations were faster than TrxA under these conditions.

Retained restriction: Zinc binding does not establish zinc as required for this reductase assay; do not generalize rate ordering to all clients or physiological concentration regimes. Original reviewer action, inherited rather than repeated here: Independently read original full Collet paper and relevant figure pages.

### Additional primary/structure context C09

scope: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

Inherited complete independent assessment: The authors could not assay a persistently oxidized zinc center because both DTT and TrxB/NADPH reduced all six cysteines.

Retained restriction: This condition was experimentally unmeasurable, not demonstrated to have no effect. Original reviewer action, inherited rather than repeated here: Independently read original full Collet paper and relevant figure pages.

### Additional primary/structure context C10

scope: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

Inherited complete independent assessment: The in vivo extent and functional consequences of zinc-center oxidation remained unresolved. Earlier work cited by the paper found all six cysteines reduced even in an oxidizing cytoplasmic background. Other zinc-dependent activities or a stress switch were hypotheses.

Retained restriction: Keep physiological uncertainty. The paper later calls OxyR a sigma factor; retain the correct transcription-regulator identity rather than repeating that error. scope: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

scope: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

scope: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

Untagged recombinant E. coli TrxC from pET11a/pJFC67, produced with zinc supplementation in the stated BL21(DE3) background; zinc-bound reduced preparation. This qualifier does not assert that zinc is essential for all TrxC catalysis.

### Qualified endpoint e034 subject

scope: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

### Qualified endpoint e035 subject

scope: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

Inherited independent scientific assessment: Full sulfane/OxyR reporter study and exact quotes support tagged-construct activation, not unqualified native persulfide occupancy. Fresh HTML QA confirms the separate display instance, table anchor and qualifier evidence.

Purified C-terminal-His OxyR expressed from pET30 in BL21(DE3), treated with hydrogen polysulfide; C199-SSH detected in vitro. C199S/C208S mutants lose reporter activation. This is distinct from the unspecified oxidized OxyR state in the peroxide abstract.

### Actual corrected complete maintained gates

representation: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

After native Codex history replacements and SSSOM regeneration, all nine maintained gates actually passed, including the complete repeated test suite (3210 passed, three skipped). Six corrected-run gates passed before a vendored fetch DNS failure; the identical network-enabled retry and remaining diff/site checks then passed against unchanged files. Both the initial SSSOM failure and later network-only wrapper failure remain historical failures.

### Actual maintained preservation and complete graph export

consistency: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

All97 adopted bodies remain exact. Every pre-scaffold file is preserved except the fixed generated pages/downloads/KGX and declared SSSOM artifact; all110 old review pairs and old committed history remain identical. Whole current KGX/downloads are byte-equal to the accepted actual THIO output:4185nodes and11734edges.

### Issue495 native actor attribution correction

provenance: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

Three provisional histories had default claude-code actor names despite Codex execution. Root retained their complete originals, native-scaffolded three explicit Codex replacements before the corrected render, verified all replacements, then removed only the checked untracked originals. Every prior committed history remained unchanged; actual all-history validation passed.

### Issue496 native source-mapping export

representation: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

The initial maintained suite found a stale SSSOM artifact. Native regeneration adds exactly three accepted THIO mappings (NADP, NADPH and proton) while preserving every old row and order; the full body equals the failed test generated expectation. The subsequent complete suite passed. No scientific record was altered to satisfy this check.

### Actual final graph/table and visual boundary

graph: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

The final rendered THIO page matches45edges, four separate qualified instances,116references and42namedidentities. The graph has44displaynodes:40base nodes plus four context instances; the two taxa remain in the component table. The final rawSVG is exactly the root visually inspected source. Dense outgoing labels overlap, so all-label legibility and browser interaction are not claimed; full table/evidence anchors are exact.

### Issue497 lossless historical evidence encoding

provenance: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY.

After the original131 receipts were copied, repository-wide Ruff collected five historical evidence scripts and reported136diagnostics. Their exact original bodies were preserved and stored as deterministic gzip; only the checked raw archive copies and corresponding portable map entries changed, alongside explicit diagnostic receipts. The unchanged repository-wide Ruff command subsequently passed. Scientific records, runtime, authorities, history and generated pages/KGX stayed byte-identical; no full-suite rerun is claimed after this archive-only correction.

## Limits And Additional Notes

- Primary abstract/metadata-only papers remain bounded. Blocked full text and the unavailable 2012 supplement are not inspected evidence; exact constructs, doses or speciation absent from accessible sources remain unknown.
- Earlier full science, author-runtime validation and pending-correction statements remain immutable historical observations. Their then-pending scientific corrections were accepted in the separately bound successors; actual current integration and maintained-gate results are assessed separately.
- This reviewer did not rerun native authority builders or full-corpus gates. Root actual execution receipts supply those results, with independent byte/preservation checks explicitly identified. Root performed both fresh whole-SQL hashing phases; this reviewer does not claim an additional whole-SQL body reread.
- Complete primary-source scientific interpretation is inherited through the sealed full review, exact unchanged fields and bounded accepted successors. This integration reviewer read the complete maintained record and successor evidence but did not reread every paper or repeat the literature search.
- Full corpus gates assess technical validity and preservation; they are not a scientific review of the other 233 records.
- The actual static whole graph and upper viewport show dense outgoing-edge label overlap. No clipping defect was confirmed. The full edge/evidence table remains the precise readable representation. No browser or interactive UI behavior was tested.
- Publication licensing and broad source exhaustion are not assessed by this review.
- The original complete review identified two scientific defects. Accepted bounded successors corrected claims M17/P06 and paragraphs16/27; issue479 subsequently corrected all12 stale native-to-authored paragraph digests. The active ledger retains its historical pending-status text, with the separately bound independent clearance and root acceptance governing the accepted final state.
- External issues are addressed through these evidence-backed assessments. No fictitious governed predecessor or previous_occurrences entry is created.
- External issues495 and496 are evidenced by their actual failed attempts and completed corrections; no fictitious governed previous_occurrences link is created. The earlier failed suite, wrong-actor scaffolds and network-only vendored/outer-wrapper failure remain historical evidence; no completion was written into either failed attempt directory. The nine-gate aggregate precedes receipt adoption. The subsequent issue497 archive-only lint failure and its lossless encoding correction/post-correction Ruff result are separately retained; every pre-receipt source body remains exact. Native review persistence occurs after this external preparation.

  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: E. coli TrxB homodimer consists of two THIOREDOXIN-REDUCT-NADPH-MONOMER
    subunits. Native complex has no direct common-name; nested activity has thioredoxin
    reductase. One FAD per subunit and electron flow NADPH to FAD to TrxB disulfide
    to thioredoxin are supported. Native E. coli B purification, recombinant K12 studies
    and inferred K12 assembly history must remain distinguished.
- assessment_id: context-M01
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context M01
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Primary abstract supports 139
    residues, 32-residue extension, four extra cysteines, recombinant RNR/insulin
    activity, DTT effect and heat sensitivity; uninspected vector/tag details stay
    unresolved.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: TrxC/Trx2 is a 139-residue second E. coli thioredoxin with an additional
    32-residue N-terminal region and four additional cysteines. Recombinant Trx2 supports
    ribonucleotide reductase with TrxB and NADPH and reduces insulin disulfides. DTT
    preincubation enhances full-length Trx2 but not the N-terminal deletion; the N-terminal
    region contributes heat sensitivity. Expression vector/tag details are unresolved
    without the primary body.
- assessment_id: context-M02
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context M02
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Full eight-page Collet paper
    read; pages3/7 also visually inspected. Twelve added contexts retain forcing conditions,
    zinc stoichiometry, untagged preparation, active-site versus metal-site distinction,
    negative assay limits and uncertain physiology.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: 'TrxC binds one zinc per protein through its four N-terminal cysteines
    with high affinity; peroxide releases zinc with conformational change. Apo-Trx2
    retains reductase activity. The physiological function of the zinc center remains
    unresolved: do not assert an established native redox switch.'
- assessment_id: context-M03
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context M03
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Primary abstract independently
    supports potentials, pKa and four-Cys-to-Ser construct. Undefined high-mass complex
    remains explicitly native prose, not a characterized assembly.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Trx2 differs from Trx1 in redox potential and active-site pKa. A four zinc-cysteine-to-serine
    construct changes potential, pKa and thermostability. Native source also reports
    undefined high-mass complexes during oxidative stress. Retain the latter as source
    context with unidentified partners, not a named stable native assembly.
- assessment_id: context-M04
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context M04
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Oxidized OxyR binding and tested
    functional overlap follow abstract; mutant peroxide resistance/diamide sensitivity
    prevent a universal antioxidant/equivalence inference.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Oxidized OxyR binds trxC promoter and activates expression. TrxA and TrxC
    overlap in tested ribonucleotide reductase, PAPS reductase and DsbC-supporting
    functions; N-terminal TrxC extension is dispensable for these tests. Double-thioredoxin
    mutants are more peroxide-resistant yet diamide-sensitive. These context-dependent
    results do not prove complete equivalence across all substrates.
- assessment_id: context-M08
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context M08
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Engineered export can make TrxA
    an oxidant in periplasm; it does not relocate native cytosolic TrxA.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Experimentally exported TrxA can act as an oxidant in the periplasm. Exported
    construct and compartment qualify this result; native cytoplasmic reductant function
    is a different state/context.
- assessment_id: context-M10
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context M10
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Early residual activity is explicitly

  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Early Cys136/139-to-Ser TrxB mutant residual activities are historical

    treated as current mechanistic evidence. Numbering differs between historical
    and full-sequence conventions.
- assessment_id: context-M11
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context M11
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Primary corrected C138S near0.1%

  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target

- assessment_id: context-M12
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context M12
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Asp139 acid-base role is proposed
    from mutants; D139N oxidative half-reaction defect and retained surrogate reaction

  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: H245N/A, D139E/N/L TrxB mutants distinguish catalytic roles. D139N impairs
    thioredoxin oxidative half-reaction despite retained surrogate pyridine-nucleotide
    transfer activity. Asp139 acid-base catalysis is proposed from these assays; do
    not identify H245 as an obligatory catalytic base.
- assessment_id: context-M14
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context M14
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Abstract plus exact1CL0 deposition

  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target

- assessment_id: context-M15
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context M15
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Original1F6M directly supports

  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science

  - scientific-clearance
  - target

- assessment_id: context-M16
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context M16
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Fluorescence equilibria depend

  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target

- assessment_id: context-M17
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context M17
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited independent context assessment with accepted successor correction.
    The catalytic cycle was modeled from wild-type TrxB flavin-oxidizing and engineered
    mixed-disulfide flavin-reducing structures. Both Arg73 and Lys36 belong to TrxA:

    by TrxB. Computed intermediates are not independently observed native turnover
    structures; source residue numbering is retained.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:

  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target

- assessment_id: context-M18
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context M18
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Homologous mycoplasma assays
    support NADPH specificity and tested heterologous failure; M129 sequences are
    exact, inferred FAD/localization are not promoted to experiments.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Partially purified M. pneumoniae and M. capricolum systems use NADPH, not
    NADH, and homologous thioredoxins; heterologous E. coli/B. subtilis thioredoxins
    fail in the tested assays. M129 TrxA102aa and TrxB315aa sequences support exact
    P75512/P75531 proteins. Oxidative-protection role is a hypothesis; no transfer
    of E. coli experimental FAD stoichiometry/structure to M129.
- assessment_id: context-M19
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context M19
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. In-vitro TrxC recycling versus
    poor metB1 trxA growth is retained with expression limitation; no universal catalytic
    incapacity/equivalence.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: TrxC can recycle methionine sulfoxide reductases in vitro, but low in vivo
    TrxC expression explains inability of a metB1 trxA mutant to grow on methionine
    sulfoxide. Do not assert physiological TrxA/TrxC equivalence or absence of TrxC
    catalytic potential.
- assessment_id: context-M20
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context M20
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Engineered active-site ring variants
    are identified separately from wild-type sequence and native core chemistry.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Active-site ring expansions/contractions and Pro-to-Ser/Lys-to-Glu substitutions
    distinguish redox activity, stability and partner binding. Cys-Gly-Arg-Pro-Cys-Lys
    retains reduced activity in vitro despite in vivo inactivity; Cys-Ala-Cys is inefficient
    and forms intermolecular disulfides. Retain engineered sequences explicitly.
- assessment_id: context-M21
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context M21
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Reduced/oxidized solution NMR
    subtle differences are preserved without contradicting study-specific earlier
    larger local differences.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Reduced and oxidized wild-type TrxA solution structures have subtle local
    differences near Cys32/Cys35, not wholesale unfolding. Earlier PMID2193685 describes
    a larger local change; report study-specific observations instead of choosing
    a universal atomic distance.
- assessment_id: context-M23
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context M23
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Oxidized crystal packing, copper
    and methylpentanediol do not establish native copper cofactor or TrxA homodimer.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Oxidized TrxA crystal contains Cys32-Cys35 disulfide and two independent
    molecules; copper and methylpentanediol support crystallization/packing. They
    are not evidence of obligatory native TrxA copper cofactors or homodimer assembly.
- assessment_id: context-M24
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context M24
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Nonnatural fluoroproline stereoisomer
    folding results are retained as engineered structure/kinetics, not core physiology.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Replacing cis-Pro76 with nonnatural (4S)-fluoroproline accelerates rate-limiting
    folding isomerization; the (4R) analog has little effect in intact structure.
    These are engineered folding observations, not new core physiological chemistry.
- assessment_id: context-R02
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context R02
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Primary abstract supports RelA/ppGpp
    stationary/starvation induction and RpoS independence in promoter fusions. Exact
    quote checked; ppGpp is regulatory condition, not substrate.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: trxA p1/p2 induction during stationary phase and amino-acid starvation
    depends on ppGpp/RelA and is RpoS-independent in tested fusion constructs. Keep
    galK promoter-reporter context; ppGpp is a regulatory condition, not a reductase
    substrate.
- assessment_id: context-R03
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context R03
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Native plus abstract support
    oxidized OxyR activation of trxC. Abstract does not chemically characterize that
    oxidized state; qualifier properly limits precision.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Native OxyR regulation of trxC has activation mode and direct promoter
    linkage with purified-protein-binding and expression evidence citations. Distinguish
    oxidized/peroxide and persulfidated OxyR states; unmodified regulator identity
    alone does not encode those states.
- assessment_id: context-R04
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context R04
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Genome-wide method abstract does
    not itself name every target. Native trxC linkage is explicitly separated from
    abstract-level methods.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Genome-wide OxyR/SoxRS reconstruction uses ChIP-exo and RNA sequencing.
    The native target link supports trxC; the abstract alone does not enumerate every
    target. Keep native annotation and primary genome-wide method attribution distinct.
- assessment_id: context-R05
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context R05
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Full original results/methods
    support His-tagged C199-persulfidation reporter activation and mutant negatives.
    C208 requirement without detected C199-C208 linkage stays unresolved.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Sulfane sulfur can persulfidate OxyR C199 and activate trxC reporters;
    sulfide alone fails. C199S/C208S and double mutants lose reporter activity, while
    four other Cys-to-Ala substitutions retain it. C208 is required without a detected
    C199-C208 crosslink; its exact role remains unresolved. OxyR is His-tagged, purified
    from BL21(DE3), and reporter/dose/aerobic state must be retained.
- assessment_id: context-R06
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context R06
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. TrxA-alone negative and TrxA+TrxB/TrxC
    overexpression signal reductions are retained as engineered sulfane-sulfur feedback;
    no uniform H2Sn chemical core reaction inferred.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: TrxA alone does not lower the sulfane-sulfur signal; co-overexpressed TrxA
    plus TrxB lowers signal during log phase. TrxC overexpression rescues the tested
    oxyR-defective sulfane-sulfur phenotype. These engineered negative-feedback observations
    do not define an additional homogeneous H2Sn core reaction.
- assessment_id: context-R07
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context R07
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Exact full-text strain/medium/time
    scopes support AB1157/PN134 induction and MG1655 weak OxyR response. No universal
    hyperoxia activation.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: '30-to-300% air-saturation oxygen switch has strain-dependent effects:
    AB1157 trxC rises2.2-fold; PN134 sodA sodB mutant trxC rises3.2-fold and growth
    slows. MG1655 shows principally SoxRS response with little OxyR response. Preserve
    parental/mutant/medium/timepoint; high oxygen is not universal strong trxC activation.'
- assessment_id: context-R08
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context R08
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Full text and actual ZIP/XLSX
    original inspected. TrxB abundance association is confounded by whole-operon deletion,
    complementation design, strain and unequal arsenite doses; no direct ArsR binding
    edge.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: TrxB abundance is lower under arsenite in AW3110 lacking arsRBC than in
    the complemented strain or MG1655. This is a proteomic association; direct ArsR
    binding/activation of trxB is not demonstrated. W3110-derived AW3110 versus MG1655,
    whole-operon deletion, altered complementation promoter and unequal matched-stress
    arsenite doses are explicit confounders.
- assessment_id: context-P01
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context P01
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. 2023 full results/methods support
    bounded low-micromolar negative damage/growth/repair results, including small
    minimal-medium advantage; not a universal absence of redoxin defense.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Physiological low-micromolar peroxide activates OxyR and induces trxC/grxA/trxB
    without broad protein-thiol damage in tested E. coli backgrounds. Hpx-minus ΔkatG
    ΔkatE ΔahpCF combined with ΔgrxA ΔtrxC showed no added growth defect under tested
    long-culture conditions and sometimes a small advantage. No substantial Pdf/Tdh/IPMI
    repair defect was found. This bounds broad antioxidant claims, not every possible
    thioredoxin role.
- assessment_id: context-P02
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context P02
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Actual Table1 and conflicting

  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Table1 gives trxC20-fold, grxA30-fold and trxB3-fold induction; body/caption
    wording differs20/30 for trxC, so avoid exact fold without table-specific provenance.
    PhoA reporter deletes its export signal residues2-22 on pAID135; residual activity
    can arise from a small periplasmic fraction. It is not native cytoplasmic PhoA.
- assessment_id: context-P03
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context P03
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. 2004 full results/methods support

  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Differential thiol trapping compares DHB4 with WP570 ΔtrxA and detects
    more modified protein spots; it does not demonstrate direct catalysis of every
    hit. Peroxide experiments use4mM H2O2 and diamide1mM, unlike2023 low-micromolar
    regimes. Preserve normalization, modified-thiol readout and indirect/compensatory
    possibilities when comparing apparent antioxidant results.
- assessment_id: context-P04
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context P04
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Proteomic association/capture

  evidence_ids:

- assessment_id: context-P06
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context P06
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment with accepted successor correction.

  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  - scientific-correction
  details: The indexed primary abstract reports DsbD beta-domain cysteine substitutions
    and alkylation-accessibility comparisons between oxidized and reduced states,

    in TM1-TM4 was unchanged, supporting a relatively static conformation while leaving
    possible changes during thioredoxin interaction unresolved. This is biochemical

- assessment_id: context-P07
  area: scope

  outcome: supported

- assessment_id: context-P08
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context P08
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. W3110/SK3981 crosslink/immunoelectron

- assessment_id: context-P09
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context P09
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. YchF oxidized dimer/TrxA ATPase

- assessment_id: context-P11
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context P11
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. IscA-dependent iron donation

  area: scope

  - target
  details: Thioredoxin and TrxB assist renaturation/protect selected unfolded proteins
    in tested assays, including cysteine-free targets. Chaperone effects are distinct
    from disulfide reduction and do not create extra core chemical steps.
- assessment_id: context-P16
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context P16
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Salmonella AhpF207/E. coli TrxB
    chimera and mutant sites are explicit; engineered AhpC specificity is not assigned
    to unmodified TrxB.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: A chimera fuses Salmonella AhpF N-terminal207aa to E. coli TrxB and gains
    AhpC reductase activity while retaining thioredoxin reductase activity. C129/132S
    and C342/345S chimeric mutants distinguish centers. Do not transfer engineered
    AhpC specificity to unmodified TrxB.
- assessment_id: context-P17
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context P17
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited independent context assessment. Actual retained primary Figure1C
    visually inspected: trxB superscriptE is present; caption defines decreased sigmaE
    secondary-screen activity. Linked supplement remains inaccessible, so exact strain/magnitude/rescue
    is not claimed.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Native source places trxB in a stress-induced mutagenesis network, but
    the retrieved primary abstract does not enumerate trxB. Keep native attribution
    and obtain the primary table before claiming directly verified trxB-specific mutagenesis
    phenotype.
- assessment_id: context-P20
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context P20
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Human negative and chimeric/L95
    results prohibit homology-only transfer of T7-specific interaction.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Human thioredoxin does not support T7 growth/processivity despite structural
    similarity. E. coli-human chimeras and L95 variants identify an interface determinant.
    Sequence homology alone is insufficient to transfer phage-processivity function
    to other thioredoxins.
- assessment_id: context-P21
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context P21
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Full TreX primary results/methods
    support reduced-TrxA chaperone function, cysteine-free target, mutant negatives
    and partial trxB deletion protection; no redox turnover per ADP-ribosylated substrate.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Cysteine-free TreX depends on reduced TrxA for folding/solubility and ART
    activity, not a TrxA redox turnover per substrate. TrxA deletion protects cells;
    trxB deletion partly protects. C33A/C36A, W32A, I61D, D62A and I76D variants impair
    binding/activity; residue numbering includes initiator relative to classic C32/C35
    papers. Distinguish E. coli and X. bovienii homologs, affinity tags and crystallographic
    complexes.
- assessment_id: context-P22
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context P22
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Full methods separate engineered
    CDI delivery, TreX mutants/affinity tags/TEV cleavage/ternary complexes and homologs.
    Negative T130C/R118C result and Scabin hypothesis remain bounded.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: TreX delivery was tested using an engineered E. coli CDI chimera carrying
    X. bovienii toxin/immunity genes, not native Xenorhabdus T6SS delivery. TreX E100A/E102A
    trapping, Strep/His tags, TEV removal and TriX-containing complexes qualify binding/structure.
    Scabin ancestry and modeled homologs are hypotheses; engineered T130C/R118C did
    not eliminate TrxA dependence.
- assessment_id: context-P23
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context P23
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Signal/export selected folding
    mutants do not change native localization identity.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Thioredoxin folding mutants selected with export reporters can permit post-translational
    export. Signal sequence and mutant folding behavior must remain explicit; they
    do not redefine native cellular location.
- assessment_id: context-P24
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context P24
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. G74S exported construct improves
    PhoA but not FlgI motility; retained substrate-specific negative result avoids
    universal oxidative gain.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Exported TrxA G74S improves PhoA oxidation but not FlgI-dependent motility;
    altered substrate specificity rather than changed redox potential explains the
    selected construct. Do not infer general gain of oxidative activity across substrates.
- assessment_id: context-G01
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context G01
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Historical E. coli B preparation
    is supported by primary metadata/native citation only; no primary body, concentration
    or MG1655 preparation assertion.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Historical purification of E. coli B thioredoxin reductase is cited natively.
    No primary body is available in this packet; strain B native preparation must
    not be silently relabeled as an experimentally purified MG1655 enzyme.
- assessment_id: context-G02
  area: scope
  topic: Original construct and experimental context G02
  outcome: supported
  summary: Inherited independent context assessment. Affinity purification abstract
    plus native011-prime strain attribution preserved; missing preparation/tag details
    not guessed.
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: Historical affinity purification is attributed to E. coli011-prime by the
    native enzyme source. Preserve exact strain and accessible metadata/abstract scope
    rather than inventing preparation tags or sequence identity.
- assessment_id: added-context-C01
  area: scope
  topic: Additional primary/structure context C01
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited complete independent assessment: Wild-type-sequence E. coli
    genomic trxC was cloned into pET11a (pJFC67), sequence verified and introduced
    into BL21(DE3)pLysS. The expression paragraph names BL21(DE3) harboring pJFC67.
    The protein was deliberately untagged, to avoid histidine-mediated metal binding.
    IPTG 0.5 mM and ZnCl2 0.8 mM were added during aerobic LB expression at 37 °C;
    sequential anion-exchange and size-exclusion purification yielded >95% pure protein.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: 'Retained restriction: Construct and metal-supplemented preparation restrictions,
    not an in vivo abundance/metal requirement. Original reviewer action, inherited
    rather than repeated here: Independently read original full Collet paper and relevant
    figure pages.'
- assessment_id: added-context-C02
  area: scope
  topic: Additional primary/structure context C02
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited complete independent assessment: Freshly purified Trx2 contained
    six reduced cysteines and approximately one zinc atom per protein. ICP-HRMS gave
    1.03 zinc/protein with no other tested metals detected. The four N-terminal Cys
    residues coordinate the metal; selective active-site oxidation by 10-fold GSSG
    at 4 °C for 10 min did not release zinc.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: 'Retained restriction: The two catalytic cysteines and four zinc-binding
    cysteines are distinct; preserve one native catalytic disulfide versus three-disulfide
    experimental preparation. Original reviewer action, inherited rather than repeated
    here: Independently read original full Collet paper and relevant figure pages.'
- assessment_id: added-context-C03
  area: scope
  topic: Additional primary/structure context C03
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited complete independent assessment: PAR alone could not extract
    zinc; thiol modification by PMPS released zinc and DTT restored binding. TPEN
    competition supports high affinity. EDTA was unsuitable for affinity competition
    because the authors report a Trx2-zinc-EDTA complex (data not shown).'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: 'Retained restriction: Do not report all chelators as equivalent or convert
    a failed competition method into an affinity measurement. Text and figure specify
    slightly different pH for the affinity experiments. Original reviewer action,
    inherited rather than repeated here: Independently read original full Collet paper
    and relevant figure pages.'
- assessment_id: added-context-C04
  area: scope
  topic: Additional primary/structure context C04
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited complete independent assessment: Completely oxidized Trx2 was
    prepared with 40-fold excess H2O2 at 43 °C for 2 h. Figure4 compared 50 µM Trx2
    with 2 mM H2O2 at 23 °C and 43 °C; zinc release and thiol oxidation were faster
    at 43 °C. Copper acceleration is described as data not shown.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: 'Retained restriction: Forcing in vitro peroxide/temperature conditions;
    physiological zinc-center oxidation and a functional redox switch remain unproven.
    Original reviewer action, inherited rather than repeated here: Independently read
    original full Collet paper and relevant figure pages.'
- assessment_id: added-context-C05
  area: scope
  topic: Additional primary/structure context C05
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited complete independent assessment: TrxB plus NADPH reduced fully
    oxidized Trx2, with NADPH consumption corresponding to all three disulfides. A
    slow two-cysteine phase preceded faster reduction of four remaining cysteines;
    assignment of the first pair to the catalytic cysteines was tentative. Zinc occupancy
    recovered to about25% without added zinc and about85% with added zinc.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: 'Retained restriction: This auxiliary three-disulfide experiment does not
    change one-disulfide native Rhea20347 stoichiometry or prove direct NADPH catalysis
    by TrxC. Original reviewer action, inherited rather than repeated here: Independently
    read original full Collet paper and relevant figure pages.'
- assessment_id: added-context-C06
  area: scope
  topic: Additional primary/structure context C06
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited complete independent assessment: Size-exclusion apparent mass
    shifted with oxidation, but analytical ultracentrifugation at 10 µM found both
    redox preparations monomeric (16,470±710 Da). SDS-PAGE showed >80% monomer with
    a minor crosslinked dimer fraction reported as data not shown.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: 'Retained restriction: Do not infer a stable native oligomer from native-gel
    bands or hydrodynamic mass. Original reviewer action, inherited rather than repeated
    here: Independently read original full Collet paper and relevant figure pages.'
- assessment_id: added-context-C07
  area: scope
  topic: Additional primary/structure context C07
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited complete independent assessment: Oxidation changed far-UV CD
    and increased protease susceptibility. The interpretation as increased alpha-helical
    structure was tentative.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: 'Retained restriction: A conformational change is supported; a complete
    experimentally resolved alternate structure is not. Original reviewer action,
    inherited rather than repeated here: Independently read original full Collet paper
    and relevant figure pages.'
- assessment_id: added-context-C08
  area: scope
  topic: Additional primary/structure context C08
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited complete independent assessment: Reduced zinc-free Trx2 retained
    the same insulin reduction rate as zinc-bound reduced Trx2 under the DTT-driven
    assay (130 µM insulin, 0.6 mM DTT, 1 µM thioredoxin, pH7.0,25 °C). Both Trx2 preparations
    were faster than TrxA under these conditions.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: 'Retained restriction: Zinc binding does not establish zinc as required
    for this reductase assay; do not generalize rate ordering to all clients or physiological
    concentration regimes. Original reviewer action, inherited rather than repeated
    here: Independently read original full Collet paper and relevant figure pages.'
- assessment_id: added-context-C09
  area: scope
  topic: Additional primary/structure context C09
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited complete independent assessment: The authors could not assay
    a persistently oxidized zinc center because both DTT and TrxB/NADPH reduced all
    six cysteines.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: 'Retained restriction: This condition was experimentally unmeasurable,
    not demonstrated to have no effect. Original reviewer action, inherited rather
    than repeated here: Independently read original full Collet paper and relevant
    figure pages.'
- assessment_id: added-context-C10
  area: scope
  topic: Additional primary/structure context C10
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited complete independent assessment: The in vivo extent and functional
    consequences of zinc-center oxidation remained unresolved. Earlier work cited
    by the paper found all six cysteines reduced even in an oxidizing cytoplasmic
    background. Other zinc-dependent activities or a stress switch were hypotheses.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: 'Retained restriction: Keep physiological uncertainty. The paper later
    calls OxyR a sigma factor; retain the correct transcription-regulator identity
    rather than repeating that error. Original reviewer action, inherited rather than
    repeated here: Independently read original full Collet paper and relevant figure
    pages.'
- assessment_id: added-context-C11
  area: scope
  topic: Additional primary/structure context C11
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited complete independent assessment: The paper summarizes earlier
    evidence that TrxC can substitute for TrxA in PAPS reductase and DsbD-related
    processes when overexpressed.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: 'Retained restriction: Secondary introduction attribution only; not a new
    experiment in this paper and not unconditional TrxA/TrxC equivalence. Original
    reviewer action, inherited rather than repeated here: Independently read original
    full Collet paper and relevant figure pages.'
- assessment_id: added-context-C12
  area: scope
  topic: Additional primary/structure context C12
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited complete independent assessment: Examples of zinc bound to an
    archaeal protein under zinc-rich crystallization and an engineered TrxA metal
    site are contrasted with the natural TrxC motif.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: 'Retained restriction: These examples do not establish a native TrxA zinc
    cofactor or widen the current E. coli/M129 pathway scope. Original reviewer action,
    inherited rather than repeated here: Independently read original full Collet paper
    and relevant figure pages.'
- assessment_id: added-context-S01
  area: scope
  topic: Additional primary/structure context S01
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited complete independent assessment: The deposited FR structure
    uses C135S TrxB and C35S TrxA (historical mature-chain numbering). Four crystallographic
    chain pairs are linked by disulfides between TrxB Cys138 and TrxA Cys32; their
    source-accession bridges are P0A9P4 and P0AA25. Current UniProt sequences include
    an initiating methionine, so the corresponding substitutions are C136S and C36S,
    respectively.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: 'Retained restriction: Engineered trapped mixed-disulfide complex, not
    a stable obligate native TrxB–TrxA assembly; retain source numbering and independently
    computed +1 mapping. Original reviewer action, inherited rather than repeated
    here: Independently read original CIF categories and replayed exact UniProt sequence
    offsets.'
- assessment_id: added-context-S02
  area: scope
  topic: Additional primary/structure context S02
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited complete independent assessment: Four FAD and four 3-aminopyridine-adenine-dinucleotide-phosphate
    molecules are deposited with four copies of each protein in the asymmetric unit.
    Two assemblies are described as tetrameric (two TrxB and two TrxA chains per assembly).'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: 'Retained restriction: The pyridine nucleotide is a non-native analogue;
    do not count the full asymmetric unit or trapped tetramer as the native TrxB homodimer
    stoichiometry. Original reviewer action, inherited rather than repeated here:
    Independently read original CIF categories and replayed exact UniProt sequence
    offsets.'
- assessment_id: added-context-S03
  area: scope
  topic: Additional primary/structure context S03
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited complete independent assessment: Issuing source and expression
    host are E. coli tax562; precise strain, vector and plasmid fields are unspecified.
    Historical database alignment positions start at1 for mature chains.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: 'Retained restriction: Do not guess an MG1655 host or silently transfer
    current full-sequence numbering into original PDB fields. Original reviewer action,
    inherited rather than repeated here: Independently read original CIF categories
    and replayed exact UniProt sequence offsets.'
- assessment_id: added-context-S04
  area: scope
  topic: Additional primary/structure context S04
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited complete independent assessment: Wild-type-sequence TrxB is
    expressed in E. coli A326, with source vector PSL350 and plasmid PTRR301. FAD
    and active-site Cys135/Cys138 disulfide were reduced by sodium dithionite after
    crystallization. Deposited biological assembly is a dimer; the asymmetric unit
    has one protein chain and one FAD.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  details: 'Retained restriction: A chemically reduced crystal preparation, not a
    captured native steady-state turnover population; source gene strain is unspecified,
    expression strain is A326. Unknown mutation field alone does not prove wild type,
    but independent exact sequence comparison supports no substitution after initiator
    removal. Original reviewer action, inherited rather than repeated here: Independently
    read original CIF categories and replayed exact UniProt sequence offsets.'

- assessment_id: qualifier-e025-subject
  area: scope
  topic: Qualified endpoint e025 subject
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited independent scientific assessment: Full Collet original and
    two exact quotes support structural zinc in untagged recombinant preparation;
    qualifier and nonessential insulin-assay result retained. Fresh HTML QA confirms
    the separate display instance, table anchor and qualifier evidence.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  - rendered-qa
  details: Untagged recombinant E. coli TrxC from pET11a/pJFC67, produced with zinc
    supplementation in the stated BL21(DE3) background; zinc-bound reduced preparation.
    This qualifier does not assert that zinc is essential for all TrxC catalysis.
- assessment_id: qualifier-e034-subject
  area: scope
  topic: Qualified endpoint e034 subject
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited independent scientific assessment: OxyR positive trxC promoter
    link and oxidized-protein abstract support edge with unresolved precise oxidized-state
    qualifier. Fresh HTML QA confirms the separate display instance, table anchor
    and qualifier evidence.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  - rendered-qa
  details: Oxidized OxyR in the peroxide-responsive promoter-binding experiment; its
    precise sulfenic-acid versus disulfide state is not assigned from this abstract
    alone.
- assessment_id: qualifier-e035-subject
  area: scope
  topic: Qualified endpoint e035 subject
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited independent scientific assessment: Full sulfane/OxyR reporter
    study and exact quotes support tagged-construct activation, not unqualified native
    persulfide occupancy. Fresh HTML QA confirms the separate display instance, table
    anchor and qualifier evidence.'
  target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:THIOREDOX-PWY
  evidence_ids:
  - prior-science
  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  - rendered-qa
  details: Purified C-terminal-His OxyR expressed from pET30 in BL21(DE3), treated
    with hydrogen polysulfide; C199-SSH detected in vitro. C199S/C208S mutants lose
    reporter activation. This is distinct from the unspecified oxidized OxyR state
    in the peroxide abstract.
- assessment_id: qualifier-e041-subject
  area: scope
  topic: Qualified endpoint e041 subject
  outcome: supported
  summary: 'Inherited independent scientific assessment: M129 Trx generic reduction
    remains by-similarity contextual assignment with reduced-state qualifier, not
    universal client equivalence. Fresh HTML QA confirms the separate display instance,
    table anchor and qualifier evidence.'
  target_ids:

  - active-coverage
  - scientific-clearance
  - target
  - rendered-qa
  details: Reduced active-site dithiol form as an electron donor; native generic source
    class scope and by-similarity function are retained. No exact stable M129 state
    accession or extra all-cysteine oxidation is inferred.

    maintained gates actually passed, including the complete repeated test suite (3210
    passed, three skipped). Six corrected-run gates passed before a vendored fetch
    DNS failure; the identical network-enabled retry and remaining diff/site checks

    network-only wrapper failure remain historical failures.

  summary: After the original131 receipts were copied, repository-wide Ruff collected
    five historical evidence scripts and reported136diagnostics. Their exact original
    bodies were preserved and stored as deterministic gzip; only the checked raw archive
    copies and corresponding portable map entries changed, alongside explicit diagnostic
    receipts. The unchanged repository-wide Ruff command subsequently passed. Scientific
    records, runtime, authorities, history and generated pages/KGX stayed byte-identical;
    no full-suite rerun is claimed after this archive-only correction.

limitations:
- Primary abstract/metadata-only papers remain bounded. Blocked full text and the
  unavailable 2012 supplement are not inspected evidence; exact constructs, doses
  or speciation absent from accessible sources remain unknown.
- Earlier full science, author-runtime validation and pending-correction statements
  remain immutable historical observations. Their then-pending scientific corrections
  were accepted in the separately bound successors; actual current integration and
  maintained-gate results are assessed separately.
- This reviewer did not rerun native authority builders or full-corpus gates. Root
  actual execution receipts supply those results, with independent byte/preservation
  checks explicitly identified. Root performed both fresh whole-SQL hashing phases;
  this reviewer does not claim an additional whole-SQL body reread.
- Complete primary-source scientific interpretation is inherited through the sealed
  full review, exact unchanged fields and bounded accepted successors. This integration
  reviewer read the complete maintained record and successor evidence but did not
  reread every paper or repeat the literature search.
- Full corpus gates assess technical validity and preservation; they are not a scientific
  review of the other 233 records.
- The actual static whole graph and upper viewport show dense outgoing-edge label
  overlap. No clipping defect was confirmed. The full edge/evidence table remains
  the precise readable representation. No browser or interactive UI behavior was tested.
- Publication licensing and broad source exhaustion are not assessed by this review.
notes:
- The original complete review identified two scientific defects. Accepted bounded
  successors corrected claims M17/P06 and paragraphs16/27; issue479 subsequently corrected
  all12 stale native-to-authored paragraph digests. The active ledger retains its
  historical pending-status text, with the separately bound independent clearance
  and root acceptance governing the accepted final state.
- External issues are addressed through these evidence-backed assessments. No fictitious
  governed predecessor or previous_occurrences entry is created.
- External issues495 and496 are evidenced by their actual failed attempts and completed
  corrections; no fictitious governed previous_occurrences link is created. The earlier
  failed suite, wrong-actor scaffolds and network-only vendored/outer-wrapper failure
  remain historical evidence; no completion was written into either failed attempt
  directory. The nine-gate aggregate precedes receipt adoption. The subsequent issue497
  archive-only lint failure and its lossless encoding correction/post-correction Ruff
  result are separately retained; every pre-receipt source body remains exact. Native
  review persistence occurs after this external preparation.
